where (i) uses the change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and (ii) leverages the boundedness of
p_{T|\bm{S}}
under Assumption
A3
, the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
) on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
, the boundedness condition on
K
under Assumption
A5
, as well as
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}\right)
with
\Upsilon_{1,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. In addition, if
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}(1)
, then the above pointwise rate of convergence can be strengthened to the uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|=o_{P}(1).

E.3.4
Analysis of
Term X
for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

We first calculate that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{nh}\cdot\textbf{Term X}\right|

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\frac{\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{h\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\right\}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K\left(u\right)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\left(u\right)\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{nh}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (ii) leverages our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the proposition statement. As a result, by Markov’s inequality, we obtain that

\sqrt{nh}\cdot\textbf{Term X}=\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}=o_{P}(1).

E.3.5
Analysis of
Term XI
for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

By direct calculations with some change of variables, we have that

Term XI

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[1-\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\bigg|\bm{S}\right]\left[\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]}{h\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(T,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\,du\right]\left[\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIa}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIb}}.

On one hand, when
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}
, we know from Assumption
A5
that
\textbf{Term XIa}=0
and

Term XIb

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)

by the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu}
under Assumption
A3
, the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
), and our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the proposition statement. Specifically, since
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
when
\bar{\mu}\neq\mu
, our assumption (c) entails that
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
.

On the other hand, when
\bar{\mu}=\mu
, we know from Assumption
A2
on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
and the boundedness of
p_{T|\bm{S}}
by Assumption
A4
that

Term XIa

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\,du\right]\left[\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right),

where we again argue from our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the proposition statement that
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
if
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\neq p_{T|\bm{S}}
and

\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1).

In addition, we have that

Term XIb

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\leq\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}K(u)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K(u)\left[uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{2})\right]^{2}\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{2})\right]^{2}}{\left[\bar{p}^{2}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+2uh\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}^{2}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{2})\right]^{2}\left[1+O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}^{2}\right)\right]}\,du\Bigg\}}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\cdot\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\cdot\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right),

where (i) applies Taylor’s expansion and uses the fact that the difference between
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
and
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
is small when
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)
as well as (i) leverages the arguments that
\sqrt{nh}\cdot h^{2}=\sqrt{nh^{5}}\to\sqrt{c_{2}}\in[0,\infty)
and
\Upsilon_{2,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
.

E.3.6
Asymptotic Normality of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

For the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
, it follows from the Lyapunov central limit theorem. Specifically, we already show in
Section E.3.1
and subsequent subsections that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh}\left[\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-m(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\psi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)+\sqrt{h}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\psi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)+o_{P}(1)

with
\psi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]
and
V_{m}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\psi_{h,t}^{2}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]=O(1)
by our calculation in
Term VI
. Then,
\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\psi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]=O(1)
and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\psi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right|^{2+c_{1}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{K^{2+c_{1}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{1}}}{n^{\frac{c_{1}}{2}}h^{1+\frac{c_{1}}{2}}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2+c_{1}}(T|\bm{S})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K^{2+c_{1}}(u)\cdot\left[\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{1}}+\mathbb{E}|Y-\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})|^{2+c_{1}}\right]}{\sqrt{(nh)^{c_{1}}}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2+c_{1}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{(nh)^{c_{1}}}}\right)=o(1)

by the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},p_{T|\bm{S}}
, the positivity condition on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
,
\mathbb{E}|Y|^{2+c_{1}}<\infty
by Assumption
A3
(c), and the requirement that
nh\to\infty
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, the Lyapunov condition holds, and we have that

\sqrt{nh}\left[\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-m(t)-h^{2}B_{m}(t)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{m}(t)\right)

after subtracting the dominating bias term
h^{2}B_{m}(t)
of
\psi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)
that we have computed in
Term VI
. The proof is thus completed.
∎

Appendix F
Proof of
Theorem 1

Theorem 1

(Consistency of estimating
\theta(t)
under positivity)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
,
A3
,
A4
,
A5
, and
A2
hold and
\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
are constructed on a data sample independent of
\{(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. For any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
, we let
\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
, and
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
be fixed bounded functions to which
\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
and
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
converge under the rates of convergence as:

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}\right),\quad\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right),

\displaystyle\text{ and }\quad\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right),

where
\Upsilon_{1,n},\Upsilon_{3,n},\Upsilon_{2,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. Then, as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)-\theta(t)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}+\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}+\Upsilon_{2,n}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

If, in addition, we assume that

(a)

\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
satisfies Assumptions
A4
and
A2
;

(b)

either (i) “
\,\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
” with only
h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\to 0
or (ii) “
\,\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}
”;

(c)

\sqrt{nh}\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]=o_{P}(1)
,

then

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\phi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

when
nh^{7}\to c_{3}
for some finite number
c_{3}\geq 0
, where

\phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right].

Furthermore,

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)-h^{2}B_{\theta}(t)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{\theta}(t)\right)

with
V_{\theta}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{h,t}^{2}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]
and

\displaystyle B_{\theta}(t)=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right\}\;\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]\quad\;\text{ when }\;\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}.\end{cases}

Remark F.1

(Uniform asymptotic theory for estimating
\theta(t)
)
.

If we assume that

\begin{cases}\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(\Upsilon_{1,n}),\\
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right),\\
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(\Upsilon_{3,n}),\\
\sqrt{nh}\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]=o_{P}(1),\end{cases}

then the pointwise convergence results in
Theorem 1
can be strengthened to the uniform ones; see our side notes in the proof below.

Proof of
Theorem 1
.

We derive the rates of convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)
given by (
5
) and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
given by (
6
) in
Section F.1
and
Section F.2
, respectively. We also prove the asymptotic linearity, double robustness, and asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
given by (
8
) in
Section F.3
.

F.1
Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)

Firstly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)
in (
5
). Under Assumption
A1
(d), we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\frac{d}{dt}\mathbb{E}\left[\mu(t,\bm{S}_{1})\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]}_{\textbf{Term I}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]}_{\textbf{Term II}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term III}}.

\bullet

Term I:
By Markov’s inequality (and Hölder’s inequality), we know that

Term I

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})\right|\right)=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right).

\bullet

Term II:
Analogously, we derive that

Term II

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right|=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

\bullet

Term III:
By the central limit theorem and the boundedness of
\beta(t,\bm{s})
on
\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}
under Assumption
A3
, we know that

\textbf{Term III}=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

As a side note, under Assumption
A3
, we know that
\left|\beta(t_{1},\bm{s})-\beta(t_{2},\bm{s})\right|\leq A_{2}|t_{1}-t_{2}|
for some absolute constant
A_{2}>0
. Together with the compactness of
\mathcal{T}
and Example 19.7 in
van der Vaart (1998)
, we also deduce that

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}\right|=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

In summary, we conclude that

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{RA}}(t)-\theta(t)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}+\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

F.2
Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)

Secondly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
in (
6
). Note that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)+\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]}_{\textbf{Term IV}}+\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}-\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}}_{\textbf{Term V}},

where
\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}
is the oracle IPW estimator of
\theta(t)
defined in (
18
) and
\beta(t,\bm{s})=\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{s})
. We shall handle
Term IV
and
Term V
in
Section F.2.1
and
Section F.2.2
, respectively.

F.2.1
Rate of Convergence of
Term IV
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)

Under Assumptions
A3
and
A5
, we calculate the bias of
\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{h}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{h}\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})\cdot p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})\,dt_{1}d\bm{s}_{1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}uK(u)\cdot\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}uK(u)\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]+\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}K(u)\cdot\frac{u^{3}h^{2}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle=O(h^{2}),

where (i) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
, (ii) applies Taylor’s expansion with some
\widetilde{t}
that lies between
t
and
t+uh
, and (iii) utilizes the properties of the second-order symmetric kernel function
K
. Similarly, we compute the variance of
\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}Y_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})\right]^{2}}\cdot Y_{i}^{2}\right]-\frac{1}{nh^{2}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}\right]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{4}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})\,dt_{1}d\bm{s}_{1}-\frac{\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}^{2}}{n}+O\left(\frac{h^{2}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t^{\prime}|\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+2uh\cdot\mu(t^{\prime\prime},\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t^{\prime\prime},\bm{s}_{1})+\sigma^{2}\right]p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}O\left(\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\right),

where (i) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and the boundedness of
\beta(t,\bm{s})
, (ii) applies the Taylor’s expansion under Assumptions
A3
and
A4
with
t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}
being two points between
t
and
t+uh
, (iii) absorbs the higher order terms to
O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)
, and (iv) utilizes the properties of
K
under Assumption
A5
and the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
). Now, by Chebyshev’s inequality and our above calculations, we obtain that

\displaystyle\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]}\right)+O(h^{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)+O(h^{2})

as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
. As a side note, under the VC-type condition on
K
(Assumption
A5
(c)), we can apply Theorem 2 in
Einmahl and Mason (2005)
to strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}\right)+O(h^{2}).

F.2.2
Rate of Convergence of
Term V
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)

By direct calculations, we have that

Term V

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}\left\{\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})+\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})\right]-\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})\right]}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]+O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\frac{O_{P}\left(\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)}{\inf_{(t,\bm{s})\in\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})-O_{P}\left(\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)\left[O(1+h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, where (i) utilizes our results for
Term IV
and Markov’s inequality.

Combining our results for
Term IV
and
Term V
, we conclude that

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}+\Upsilon_{2,n}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

F.3
Asymptotic Properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

Finally, using the similar arguments to
Section E.3
, we establish the asymptotic properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
in (
8
). Under Assumption
A1
, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[Y_{i}-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-t)\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]+h\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]+\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{P}_{n}\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]}_{\textbf{Term VI}}+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]}_{\textbf{Term VII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term VIII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term IX}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term X}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\left[1-\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIa}}+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIb}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIc}},

where
\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\mu,\beta,p_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\mu(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]+\beta(t,\bm{S})
. It remains to show that the dominating
Term VI
is of order
O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
in
Section F.3.1
and the remainder terms are of order
o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
for any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
in
Section F.3.2
,
Section F.3.3
, and
Section F.3.4
, and
Section F.3.5
. We shall also derive the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
in
Section F.3.6
.

F.3.1
Analysis of
Term VI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

We analyze the variance and bias of
Term VI
separately as follows. By direct calculations, we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\textbf{Term VI}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}\left[\mathbb{P}_{n}\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]+h\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{1}{nh^{2}}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t_{1}|\bm{s}_{1})}\left\{\sigma^{2}+\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-(t_{1}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}\right\}p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})\,dt_{1}d\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left\{\sigma^{2}+\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}\right\}p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})}\cdot\left\{\sigma^{2}+\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}+O(h^{2})\right\}\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h)\right]\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}O\left(\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\right)

where (i) uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality on the covariance, (ii) leverages the boundedness of
\bar{\beta}
under Assumption
A3
to derive the term
O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)
, (iii) applies a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
, as well as (iv) utilizes the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\beta}
under Assumption
A3
and the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
) on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
. In the above calculation, we also note from the line (i) that the second part
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})
of
\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)
is of smaller order than the first term
\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]
. Thus, we can only keep the first term in the final asymptotically linear form of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
. Now, by Chebyshev’s inequality, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left[\mathbb{P}_{n}\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\mathbb{P}_{n}\phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where
\phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]
. In addition, by direct calculations and Taylor’s expansions, we derive that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Bias}\left[\textbf{Term VI}\right]

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left[\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(t_{1}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right)+hu\left(\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{2\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{3}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{6\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{3}(t|\bm{S})}+O(h^{4})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right)+hu\left(\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\mu(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-1\right]\right\}+h^{2}B_{\theta}(t)+O(h^{3}),

where (i) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and (ii) applies Taylor’s expansion. Here, the complicated bias term
B_{\theta}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle B_{\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\Bigg\{\frac{\left[\mu(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}

\displaystyle\quad\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\hskip 42.67912pt+\frac{6\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{3\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\Bigg\{\frac{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+2p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}+\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{3\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right].

Under the condition that either
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
or
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}
, we have that

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\left[\mu(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-1\right]\right\}=0

and

\displaystyle B_{\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}+\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{3\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\,\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]\quad\text{ when }\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}},\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right\}\,\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]\quad\quad\text{ when }\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}.\end{cases}

As a result, as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, we know that

Term VI

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=h^{2}B_{\theta}(t)+o(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

As a side note, under the VC-type condition on the kernel function
K

(
Einmahl and Mason, 2005
)
(Assumption
A5
(c)), we can strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the following uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\textbf{Term VI}\right|=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}\right);

see Theorem 4 in
Einmahl and Mason (2005)
for details.

F.3.2
Analysis of
Term VII
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

By Markov’s inequality, we know that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term VII}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{h^{3}}\cdot\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{h^{3}}\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\right)=o_{P}(1)

because
\mathbb{E}\left\{h^{3}\cdot\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}=h^{3}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}=O_{P}\left(h^{3}\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}^{2}\right)
and
h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. As a side note, under Assumption
A3
on
\bar{\beta}
and
\widehat{\beta}
, we know that the function
\bm{s}\mapsto\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
is Lipschitz continuous with respect to
t\in\mathcal{T}
. Together with the compactness of
\mathcal{T}
and Example 19.7 in
van der Vaart (1998)
, we can also deduce that

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\sqrt{h^{3}}\cdot\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right|=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{h^{3}}\cdot\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right),

which will be
o_{P}(1)
as well if
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{h}\right)
.

F.3.3
Analyses of
Term VIII
and
Term IX
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

The argument for showing
Term VIII
and
Term IX
to be
o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
will be similar to the one for
Term VII
above. By Markov’s inequality, we know that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term VIII}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)=o_{P}(1)

because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\left(\mu(T,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\left[\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses the change of variable
u=\frac{T-t}{h}
in the integration, (ii) leverages the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta}
under Assumption
A3
and the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
) on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
, as well as (iii) applies
\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)
with
\Upsilon_{2,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. As a side note again, under the VC-type condition on the kernel function
K

(
Einmahl and Mason, 2005
)
and
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}(1)
, we can strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the following uniform result as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|=o_{P}(1).

Similarly, by Markov’s inequality, we have that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IX}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\max\left\{\Upsilon_{1,n},h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\right\}\right)=o_{P}(1)

because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(t_{1}-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}dt_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+hu\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}du\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}+h^{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}^{2}+h^{2}\Upsilon_{3,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses the change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and (ii) leverages the boundedness of
p_{T|\bm{S}}
under Assumption
A3
, the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
) on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
, the boundedness condition on
K
under Assumption
A5
, as well as
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}\right)
and
h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\right)
with
\Upsilon_{1,n},h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. In addition, if
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}(1)
and
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{h}\right)
, then the above pointwise rate of convergence can be strengthened to the uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}\right|=o_{P}(1).

F.3.4
Analysis of
Term X
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

We first calculate that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term X}\right|

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{h\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(T|\bm{S})}\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}\right\}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K\left(u\right)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+hu\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{nh}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (ii) leverages our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement. As a result, by Markov’s inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term X}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\right]}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

F.3.5
Analysis of
Term XI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

By direct calculations with some change of variables, we have that

Term XI

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\,du\right]\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIa}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\,du\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIb}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K(u)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIc}}.

On one hand, when
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}
, we know from Assumption
A5
that
\textbf{Term XIa}=\textbf{Term XIb}=0
and

Term XIc

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K(u)\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h}\cdot\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)

by the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu}
under Assumption
A3
, the positivity condition (Assumption
A2
), and our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement. Specifically, since
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
when
\bar{\mu}\neq\mu
and
\bar{\beta}\neq\beta
, our assumption (c) ensures that
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
.

On the other hand, when
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
, we know from Assumption
A2
on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
and the boundedness of
p_{T|\bm{S}}
by Assumption
A4
that

Term XIa

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[1-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\,du\right]\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where we argue from our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement that
\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
if
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\neq p_{T|\bm{S}}
and
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
. In addition, we also have that

Term XIb

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where we again argue from our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement that
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
if
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\neq p_{T|\bm{S}}
and
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
. Finally, we also derive that

Term XIc

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K(u)\left[\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})-\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\times\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{uK(u)\left[\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{3})\right]\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{2})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\left[\bar{p}^{2}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+2uh\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}^{2}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{2})\right]\left[1+O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}\right)\right]}\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-p_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\cdot\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where (i) applies Taylor’s expansion and mean-value theorem for integrals as well as uses the fact that the difference between
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
and
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
is small when
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})-\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(u|\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{2,n}\right)
, while (ii) leverages the arguments that
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot h^{2}=\sqrt{nh^{7}}\to\sqrt{c_{3}}\in[0,\infty)
and
\Upsilon_{2,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
.

F.3.6
Asymptotic Normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)

For the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
, it follows from the Lyapunov central limit theorem. Specifically, we already show in
Section F.3.1
and subsequent subsections that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\phi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)+o_{P}(1)

with

\phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

and
V_{\theta}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{h,t}^{2}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]=O(1)
by our calculation in
Term VI
. Then,

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\phi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]=O(1)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\phi_{h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right|^{2+c_{1}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2+c_{1}}K^{2+c_{1}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{1}}}{n^{\frac{c_{1}}{2}}h^{1+\frac{c_{1}}{2}}\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2+c_{1}}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2+c_{1}}(T|\bm{S})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2+c_{1}}K^{2+c_{1}}(u)\cdot\left[\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{1}}+\mathbb{E}|Y|^{2+c_{1}}\right]}{\sqrt{(nh)^{c_{1}}}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2+c_{1}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{(nh)^{c_{1}}}}\right)=o(1)

by the boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},p_{T|\bm{S}}
, the positivity condition on
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
,
\mathbb{E}|Y|^{2+c_{1}}<\infty
by Assumption
A3
(c), and the requirement that
nh^{3}\to\infty
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, the Lyapunov condition holds, and we have that

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)-h^{2}B_{\theta}(t)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{\theta}(t)\right)

after subtracting the dominating bias term
h^{2}B_{\theta}(t)
of
\phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)
that we have computed in
Term VI
. The proof of
Theorem 1
is thus completed.
∎

F.4
Asymptotic Bias of A Naive AIPW Estimator of
\theta(t)

Naively, one may combine the RA estimator (
5
) with the IPW estimator (
6
) to derive an augmented IPW (AIPW) estimator of
\theta(t)
with the following (or other similar) form as:

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]+h\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right\}.

(40)

However, this naive AIPW estimator is not doubly robust as demonstrated by Proposition
F.1
below—it is only robust to the misspecification of the limiting quantity of
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
. In other words,
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)
will be asymptotically unbiased only when the estimated conditional density
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
converges to the true conditional density
p_{T|\bm{S}}
in a certain rate.

Analogous to our calculations in
Section F.3
, we can decompose
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)-\theta(t)
under Assumption
A1
as:

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\frac{Y_{i}}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]+h\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right\}-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]+\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\Phi_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{P}_{n}\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]}_{\textbf{Dominating Term}}+\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\frac{Y}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\left[1-\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\bar{p}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]+\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\left[\frac{1}{\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\right]\left[\frac{Y}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\},

where
\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\beta,p_{T|\bm{S}}\right)=\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\frac{Y}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]+\beta(t,\bm{S})
. Thus, in order to study the asymptotically dominating bias of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)
in (
40
), it suffices to compute
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]
as in Proposition
F.1
below.

Proposition F.1

(Asymptotically dominating bias of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)
)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
,
A2
,
A3
,
A4
, and
A5
hold as well as
\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
are constructed on a data sample independent of
\{(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. For any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
, we let
\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
, and
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
be fixed bounded functions to which
\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
and
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
converge. Assume also that
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}
satisfies Assumptions
A4
and
A2
. Then, the asymptotically dominating bias of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{AIPW}}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-1\right]\right\}+O(h^{2})

when
h\to 0
and
n\to\infty
.

Proof of Proposition
F.1
.

By direct calculations under Assumption
A1
, we derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Phi}_{h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}\right)-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h^{2}}\right)-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K\left(u\right)}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\frac{u\cdot\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{K\left(u\right)\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\frac{u\cdot\mu(t,\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}+\frac{u^{2}\beta(t,\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}}-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{4}h^{2}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{3})\right]\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}K\left(u\right)\left[p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})+O(h^{4})\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{2\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{3}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{6\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{3}(t|\bm{S})}+O(h^{4})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\frac{u\cdot\mu(t,\bm{S})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}+\frac{u^{2}\beta(t,\bm{S})}{\kappa_{2}}-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{3}h}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+\frac{u^{4}h^{2}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{3})\right]\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-1\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+h^{2}\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)+O(h^{3}),

where (i) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
while (ii) and (iii) apply Taylor’s expansions under Assumptions
A4
and
A3
. Here, the complicated bias term
\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\Bigg\{\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg[\frac{1}{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+\frac{6\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{3\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\Bigg\{\frac{\left[\frac{\kappa_{4}}{\kappa_{2}}\cdot\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})-2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+2p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\right]^{2}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t|\bm{S})}+\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})}{3\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right].

When
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}=p_{T|\bm{S}}
, we have that

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\right\}+\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\left[\beta(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}-1\right]\right\}=0

and
\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{S})\right]
. In this case, the dominating bias term is
h^{2}\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)
, which tends to 0 as
h\to 0
and
n\to\infty
.

However, when
\bar{\beta}=\beta
(and
\bar{\mu}=\mu
), the dominating bias is equal to

\mathbb{E}_{\bm{S}}\left\{\frac{\mu(t,\bm{S})\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\left[\frac{p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}{\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{S})}\right]\right\}+h^{2}\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t),

which is not necessarily 0.

This also shows that the naive AIPW estimator (
40
) is not doubly robust.
∎

Appendix G
Proof of
Theorem 2

Theorem 2

(Efficient influence function)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A2
,
A3
, and
A5
hold for the nonparametric model containing
\mathrm{P}
. For any fixed bandwidth
h>0
and
t\in\mathcal{T}
, the efficient influence function of
\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})
relative to this model is given by

\displaystyle\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})

\displaystyle=\beta(t,\bm{S})+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})+h^{2}\cdot\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t),

where
\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})+o(h)
. Under the setup of
Theorem 1
, the resulting estimator of
\theta(t)
can be written as:

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[Y_{i}-\widehat{\mu}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right]+h\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right\},

which has the same doubly robust properties and asymptotic variance as our proposed one
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
.

Proof of
Theorem 2
.

We start with a parametric submodel
(
