van der Vaart, 1998
;
Kennedy, 2024
)
with a family of density functions defined as:

p_{\epsilon}(y,t,\bm{s})=p(y,t,\bm{s})\left[1+\epsilon\cdot g(y,t,\bm{s})\right],

where
g:\mathcal{Y}\times\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}\to\mathbb{R}
is a mean-zero function
\int g(y,t,\bm{s})\cdot p(y,t,\bm{s})\,dydtd\bm{s}=0
such that
p_{\epsilon}(y,t,\bm{s})\geq 0
for any
\epsilon\in\mathbb{R}
close to 0. We will also suppress the subscripts for density functions, such as denoting
p_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
by
p(t|\bm{s})
. The efficient influence function for
\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})
is the mean-zero function that coincides with the canonical gradient
\lim\limits_{\epsilon\to 0}\frac{\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}_{\epsilon})-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}{\epsilon}
. Under this parametric submodel, we know that

\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}_{\epsilon})=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p_{\epsilon}(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\,dydt_{1}d\bm{s}\quad\text{ with }\quad p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})=\frac{p_{\epsilon}(t_{1},\bm{s})}{p_{\epsilon}(\bm{s})}.

Then, we have that

\displaystyle\begin{split}&\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}_{\epsilon})-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})\\
&=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p_{\epsilon}(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\,dydt_{1}d\bm{s}-\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\,dydt_{1}d\bm{s}\\
&=\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{\epsilon}(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\left[\frac{1}{p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\right]dydt_{1}d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term I}}\\
&\quad+\underbrace{\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\left[p_{\epsilon}(y,t_{1},\bm{s})-p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\right]dydt_{1}d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term II}}.\end{split}

(41)

Now, by Taylor’s expansion, we compute that

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}

\displaystyle=\frac{p_{\epsilon}(\bm{s})}{p_{\epsilon}(t_{1},\bm{s})}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}

\displaystyle=\frac{p(\bm{s})+\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})dy_{2}dt_{2}}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})+\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\,dy_{2}}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}

\displaystyle=\frac{p(\bm{s})}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}+\epsilon\cdot\frac{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},\bm{s},t_{2})\cdot g(y_{2},\bm{s},t_{2})dy_{2}dt_{2}}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})}

\displaystyle\quad-\epsilon\cdot\frac{p(\bm{s})}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},\bm{s},t_{1})\cdot g(y_{2},\bm{s},t_{1})\,dy_{2}+o(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=\epsilon\left[\frac{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})dy_{2}dt_{2}}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})}-\frac{p(\bm{s})}{p(t_{1},\bm{s})^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\,dy_{2}\right]+o(\epsilon).

Thus, we know that

Term I

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\left[1+O(\epsilon)\right]\left[\frac{1}{p_{\epsilon}(t_{1}|\bm{s})}-\frac{1}{p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\right]dydt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1},\bm{s})}\left[\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\,dy_{2}dt_{2}\right]dydt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad-\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\left[\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\,dy_{2}\right]dydt_{1}d\bm{s}+o(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{s})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}p(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{2},\bm{s})\,dt_{2}dy_{2}\right]dt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad-\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{s})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot g(y_{2},t_{1},\bm{s})\,dy_{2}dt_{1}d\bm{s}+o(\epsilon)

\displaystyle=\epsilon\left\langle\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}dt_{1}-\frac{\mu(T,\bm{S})\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})},\,g(Y,\bm{S},T)\right\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}+o(\epsilon),

where
\big\langle f(Y,T,\bm{S}),\,g(Y,\bm{S},T)\big\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}=\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}f(y,t,\bm{s})\cdot g(y,t,\bm{s})\cdot p(y,t,\bm{s})\,dydtd\bm{s}
.
Furthermore,

Term II

\displaystyle=\epsilon\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\cdot g(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\,dydt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\epsilon\left\langle\frac{Y\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})},\,g(Y,\bm{S},T)\right\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}.

Thus, we know that

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\frac{\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}_{\epsilon})-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}{\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})},\,g(Y,\bm{S},T)\right\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}.

In addition,
\left\langle C_{0},g(Y,\bm{S},T)\right\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}=0
for any constant
C_{0}
due to the constraint
\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}g(y,t,\bm{s})\cdot p(y,t,\bm{s})\,dydtd\bm{s}=0
. Hence,

\displaystyle\lim_{\epsilon\to 0}\frac{\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}_{\epsilon})-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})}{\epsilon}

\displaystyle=\left\langle\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}+C_{0},\,g(Y,\bm{S},T)\right\rangle_{L_{2}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})},

and the efficient influence function is

L^{*}(Y,\bm{S},T)=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}+C_{0}.

To determine
C_{0}
, we use the fact that
\mathbb{E}\left[L^{*}(Y,\bm{S},T)\right]=0
, which implies that

\displaystyle C_{0}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{-\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle=0-\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{s})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p(t_{1},\bm{s})\,dt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=-\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{Y}}\frac{y\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(t_{1}|\bm{s})}\cdot p(y,t_{1},\bm{s})\,dydt_{1}d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=-\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\frac{Y\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}\right]

\displaystyle=-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}).

Therefore, the final efficient influence function for
\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})=\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\frac{Y\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}\right]
for any fixed
h>0
and
t\in\mathcal{T}
is

\displaystyle L^{*}(Y,\bm{S},T)

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,dt_{1}+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})\cdot u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,du+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\mu(t,\bm{s})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{s})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})+o(h^{3})\right]du

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})

\displaystyle=\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{s})+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})+\frac{h^{2}\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})+o(h^{2})

\displaystyle=\beta(t,\bm{s})+\frac{\left[Y-\mu(T,\bm{S})\right]\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T|\bm{S})}-\varpi_{h,t}(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$})+h^{2}\cdot\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t),

where
\widetilde{B}_{\theta}(t)=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})+o(h)
. This gives rise to another doubly robust estimator of
\theta(t)
as:

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[Y_{i}-\widehat{\mu}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right]+h\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right\}.

One can follow our arguments in the proof of
Theorem 1
to derive the same asymptotic properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)
. In particular, we will demonstrate that
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
in (
8
) and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)
above have the same asymptotic variance.

By our arguments in the proof of
Theorem 1
, we know that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]=\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right]}_{\textbf{Term III}}+o_{P}(1)

and

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]=\underbrace{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]}_{\textbf{Term IV}}+o_{P}(1),

where
\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
, and
\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
are fixed bounded functions to which
\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
and
\widehat{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t|\bm{s})
converge. Thus, the asymptotic variances of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)
and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
are given by
\mathrm{Var}\left(\textbf{Term III}\right)
and
\mathrm{Var}\left(\textbf{Term IV}\right)
, respectively. We compute their difference as follows:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left(\textbf{Term III}\right)-\mathrm{Var}\left(\textbf{Term IV}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t_{1},\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t_{1},\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(t_{1}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(t_{1}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t_{1}|\bm{S})\,dt_{1}\right\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}(u)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t+uh,\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sqrt{h}\cdot u\cdot K(u)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t+uh,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\left[\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sqrt{h}\cdot u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}(u)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+O(h^{3})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\quad-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}\cdot K^{2}\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}+O(h^{3})

\displaystyle=-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{h^{2}\cdot u^{4}\cdot K^{2}\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}^{2}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+O(h^{3})

\displaystyle=O(h^{2}),

where (i) uses the second-order kernel property with
\int_{\mathbb{R}}u\cdot K(u)\,du=0
to argue that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sqrt{h}\cdot u\cdot K\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle\asymp\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{\sqrt{h}\cdot u\cdot K(u)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t+uh,\bm{S})\right]p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{h^{3}}\right).

As a result, the asymptotic variances of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)
and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
are indeed the same when
n\to\infty
and
h\to 0
.

Notice that if the regression model is correctly specified,
i.e.
,
\bar{\mu}=\mu,\bar{\beta}=\beta
, then the differences between the asymptotic variances of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR,2}}(t)
and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{DR}}(t)
will be of smaller order as
O(h^{4})
.
∎

Appendix H
Proofs of Propositions
3
,
4
, and
5

H.1
Proof of Proposition
3

Proposition 3

(Inconsistency of IPW estimators)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
(a-c),
A3
,
A4
(c), and
A5
(a-b) hold under the additive confounding model (
13
). Assume also that when the bandwidth
h
is small, the Lebesgue measure of the symmetric difference set satisfies

\left|\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\triangle\mathcal{S}(t)\right|=\left|\left[\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\right]\cup\left[\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\right]\right|=o(1)

for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and
u\in\mathbb{R}
. Then, when
h
is small, the expectation of
\widetilde{m}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
in (
18
) is given by

\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\widetilde{m}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]=\bar{m}(t)\cdot\rho(t)+\omega(t)+o(1),

where
\rho(t)=\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left(\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\right)
and
\omega(t)=\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\eta(\bm{S})\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\}}\right]
. If, in addition, there exists a constant
A_{h}>0
depending on
h
such that

\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{S})\right]\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\}}\right]\right\}u\cdot K(u)\,du=O(A_{h})

for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and
u\in\mathbb{R}
when
h
is small, then the expectation of
\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
in (
12
) is given by

\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)\cdot\rho(t)+O\left(\frac{A_{h}}{h}\right).

Proof of Proposition
3
.

Notice that the conditional density support
\mathcal{S}(t)
depends on
t
when the positivity condition is violated. Under the additive confounding model (
13
), we have that

\displaystyle\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\widetilde{m}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\frac{1}{h}\frac{K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\cdot Y_{i}\right]

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\frac{1}{h}\frac{K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\bar{m}(T_{i})+\eta(\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{t})}\frac{1}{h}\frac{K(\frac{\widetilde{t}-t}{h})}{p_{T|\bm{S}}(\widetilde{t}|\bm{s})}\left[\bar{m}(\widetilde{t})+\eta(\bm{s})\right]p(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{t})}\frac{1}{h}K\left(\frac{\widetilde{t}-t}{h}\right)\left[\bar{m}(\widetilde{t})+\eta(\bm{s})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}K\left(u\right)\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}K(u)\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}du

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}K(u)\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}K(u)\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}du

\displaystyle\quad-\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}K(u)\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})\right]\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s})d\bm{s}+O(h^{2})+o(1)

\displaystyle=\bar{m}(t)\cdot\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}(\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t))+\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\eta(\bm{S})\cdot\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\}}\right]+o(1)

\displaystyle=\bar{m}(t)\cdot\rho(t)+\omega(t)+o(1),

where (i) follows from a change of variable
u=\frac{\widetilde{t}-t}{h}
, (ii) is due to Taylor’s expansion under Assumption
A3
, and (iii) relies on our assumption on the Lebesgue measure
\left|\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\triangle\mathcal{S}(t)\right|=o(1)
for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and
u\in\mathbb{R}
.

Similarly, we can also derive that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\frac{\left[\bar{m}(T_{i})+\eta(\bm{S}_{i})\right]\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p_{T|\bm{S}}(T_{i}|\bm{S}_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(v)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]u\cdot K(u)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})\right]u\cdot K(u)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(vi)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)\cdot p_{S}(\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}+O(h)

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{S})\right]\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\}}\right]\right\}u\cdot K(u)\,du

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{\kappa_{2}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\}}\right]\right\}u^{2}K(u)\,du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(vii)}}}{{=}}\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)\cdot\rho(t)+O\left(\frac{A_{h}}{h}\right),

where (iv) follows from a change of variable
u=\frac{\widetilde{t}-t}{h}
, (v) is due to Taylor’s expansion under Assumption
A3
, (vi) leverages the property of the second-order kernel
K
, and (vii) relies on our assumptions on the Lebesgue measure
\left|\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\triangle\mathcal{S}(t)\right|=o(1)
and (
19
) for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and
u\in\mathbb{R}
. The results follow.
∎

H.2
Proof of Proposition
4

Proposition 4
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
(a-c),
A3
,
A4
(c), and
A5
(a-b) hold under the additive confounding model (
13
). Then, when the bandwidth
h
is small, the expectation of the modified IPW quantity (
20
) is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{S})\right]\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\}}\right]\Big|T=t\right\}u\cdot K(u)\,du.

Proof of Proposition
4
.

Recall that the conditional density support
\mathcal{S}(t)
depends on
t
when the positivity condition fails to hold. Under the additive confounding model (
13
), we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\left[\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+O\left(h^{3}\right)\right]u\cdot K(u)\cdot p(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\left[\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)}-\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\right]\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K(u)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du\right\}

\displaystyle=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left\{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{S})\right]\left[\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t)\}}-\mathbbm{1}_{\{\bm{S}\in\mathcal{S}(t)\setminus\mathcal{S}(t+uh)\}}\right]\Big|T=t\right\}u\cdot K(u)\,du,

where (i) follows from a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
, (ii) is due to Taylor’s expansion, and (iii) utilizes the fact that
K
is a second-order kernel function by Assumption
A5
(b). The result thus follows.

As stated in Remark
6
, we should evaluate the conditional density
p_{\bm{S}|T}
at the (query) point
(t,\bm{S})
instead of the sample point
(T,\bm{S})
in the modified IPW quantity (
20
). To see this, we consider the alternative modified IPW quantity

\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,2}(Y,T,\bm{S})=\frac{Y\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{S}|T)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T,\bm{S})}

and compute its expectation as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,2}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t_{1})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t+uh)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(v)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+O(h^{3})\right]u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t+uh)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(vi)}}}{{=}}\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\cdot u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot p_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}_{1}|t+uh)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+O(h^{2})+\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\eta(\bm{S})|T=t_{1}\right]\,dt_{1},

where (i) follows from a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
, (ii) is due to Taylor’s expansion, and (iii) utilizes the fact that
K
is a second-order kernel function by Assumption
A5
(b). Hence, it is unclear how we can eliminate the additional bias term
\int_{\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\eta(\bm{S})|T=t_{1}\right]\,dt_{1}
from
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,2}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]
unless
\mbox{$\mathbb{E}$}\left[\eta(\bm{S})|T=t\right]=0
, which is not true in general.
∎

H.3
Proof of Proposition
5

Proposition 5
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
(a-c),
A3
,
A4
(c),
A5
(a-b), and
A6
hold under the additive confounding model (
13
). Then, when the bandwidth
h>0
is small, the expectation of the modified IPW quantity (
21
) is given by

\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+\frac{h^{2}\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)+O\left(h^{3}\right).

Proof of Proposition
5
.

Since the kernel function
K
has a compact support under Assumption
A5
, we can assume, without loss of generality, that it is supported on
[-1,1]
. Then, when
h<\frac{\zeta}{A_{0}}
(or, equivalently
A_{0}|uh|<\zeta
for any
u\in[-1,1]
), we have that

\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta\subset\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\left(A_{0}|uh|\right)\subset S(t+uh)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathcal{L}_{\zeta}(t)\subset\mathcal{L}_{A_{0}|\delta|}(t)\subset\mathcal{S}(t+\delta)

by Assumption
A6
. Then, under model (
13
) and the support shrinking approach for
p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
, the expectation of
\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})
is given by

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t_{1})+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\cdot u\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t+uh)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\cdot u\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)+O\left(h^{4}\right)\right]u\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{\left[\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s}_{1})\right]\cdot u\cdot K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot h}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du}_{=0}+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)\cdot u^{2}K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{h\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)\cdot u^{3}K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{2\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du}_{=0}+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{h^{2}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)\cdot u^{4}K(u)\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{6\kappa_{2}}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(h^{3}\right)

\displaystyle=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)+\frac{h^{2}\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)+O\left(h^{3}\right)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t,\bm{S})\Big|T=t\right]+\frac{h^{2}\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)+O\left(h^{3}\right),

where (i) uses the definition of the
\zeta
-interior conditional density (
23
) and (ii) follows from the fact that
K
is a second-order kernel function under Assumption
A5
(b). The result under the level set approach for
p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
follows from almost identical arguments.
∎

Appendix I
Proof of
Theorem 6

Theorem 6

(Consistency of estimating
\theta(t)
without positivity)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
(a-c),
A3
,
A4
,
A5
, and
A6
hold under the additive confounding model (
13
), and the support
\mathcal{S}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
of the marginal density
p_{\bm{S}}
is compact. In addition,
\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{\zeta},\widehat{p}
are constructed on a data sample independent of
\{(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. For any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
, we let
\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
, and
\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})
be fixed bounded functions to which
\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
, and
\widehat{p}(t,\bm{s})
converge under the rates of convergence as:

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right),\quad\sup_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{4,n}\right),

\displaystyle\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}\right),\quad\text{ and }\quad\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{6,n}\right),

where
\Upsilon_{3,n},\Upsilon_{4,n},\Upsilon_{5,n},\Upsilon_{6,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. Then, as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-\theta(t)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}+\Upsilon_{4,n}+\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right),

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}+\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

If, in addition, we assume that

(a)

\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}
satisfy Assumptions
A4
and
A6
as well as
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\Upsilon_{5,n}=o(1)
;

(b)

either (i) “
\,\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
” or (ii) “
\,\bar{p}=p
”;

(c)

\sqrt{nh}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]=o_{P}(1)
,

then

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\left[\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\theta(t)\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

when
nh^{7}\to c_{3}
for some finite number
c_{3}\geq 0
, where

\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right].

Furthermore,

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)-h^{2}B_{C,\theta}(t)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{C,\theta}(t)\right)

with
V_{C,\theta}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{C,h,t}^{2}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]
and

\displaystyle B_{C,\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\int\left\{\frac{3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+p(t,\bm{s})\left[\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)\right]}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\right\}\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}&\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)&\text{ when }\bar{p}=p.\end{cases}

Proof of
Theorem 6
.

We derive the rates of convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
given by (
16
) and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{IPW}}(t)
given by (
25
) in
Section I.1
and
Section I.2
, respectively. We also prove the asymptotic linearity, double robustness, and asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
given by (
26
) in
Section I.3
.

I.1
Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)

Firstly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
in (
16
). By Proposition
C.1
, we know that
\theta(t)=\bar{m}^{\prime}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(T,\bm{S})\Big|T=t\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(T,\bm{S})\big|T=t\right]
and

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\int\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\,d\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\,dF_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]d\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)}_{\textbf{Term I}}+\underbrace{\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\,d\left[\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)\right]}_{\textbf{Term II}}+\underbrace{\int\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\beta(t,\bm{s})\right]dF_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)}_{\textbf{Term III}}.

\bullet

Term I:
By Markov’s inequality (and Hölder’s inequality), we know that

\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})\right|=O_{P}\left(\mathbb{E}\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{1})\right|\right)=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right)

for any random vector
\bm{S}_{1}
supported on
\mathcal{S}\subset\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Thus,

\textbf{Term I}\leq\int\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|d\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right).

\bullet

Term II:
By the compactness of
\mathcal{S}
and the fact that
\mathcal{S}(t)\subset\mathcal{S}
, we know that the Lebesgue measure
|\mathcal{S}(t)|
satisfies
|\mathcal{S}(t)|\leq|\mathcal{S}|<\infty
for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and thus,

Term II

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|\cdot\left|\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)\right|\right|_{\mathrm{TV}}

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|\cdot\sup_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)\right|\cdot|\mathcal{S}|

\displaystyle=O_{P}(\Upsilon_{4,n})

under Assumption
A3
and the condition that
\sup\limits_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{4,n}\right)
, where
\left|\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)\right|\right|_{\mathrm{TV}}
is the total variation distance between the probability measures associated with
\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)
and
F_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)
. Notice that
\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\cdot|t)
can be constructed on the same data sample
\left\{(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right\}_{i=1}^{n}
.

\bullet

Term III:
Similar to the argument for
Term I
, we have that

\textbf{Term III}\leq\int\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\beta(t,\bm{s})\right|d\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

In summary, we conclude that

\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-\theta(t)=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}+\Upsilon_{4,n}+\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

I.2
Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)

Secondly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)
in (
25
). Recall from (
21
) that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\theta(t)+\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}-\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(T,\bm{S})\big|T=t\right]}_{\textbf{Term IV}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]}_{\textbf{Term V}}.

We shall handle
Term IV
and
Term V
in
Section I.2.1
and
Section I.2.2
, respectively.

I.2.1
Rate of Convergence of
Term IV
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)

We already computed in Proposition
5
that

\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\theta(t)=\frac{h^{2}\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)+O\left(h^{3}\right)

under model (
13
). In particular, the above equality holds true even when we replace the true
\zeta
-interior conditional density
p_{\zeta}
with the limiting
\zeta
-interior conditional density
\bar{p}_{\zeta}
because
\bar{p}_{\zeta}
also satisfies Assumptions
A4
and
A6
. Additionally, we calculate the variance of
\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]
under
\bar{p}_{\zeta}
as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{4}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{4}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}^{2}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{p^{2}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]-\frac{1}{nh^{4}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{4}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}\times\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\,dt_{1}d\bm{s}_{1}-\frac{\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(T,\bm{S})\big|T=t\right]\right\}^{2}}{n}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{h^{2}}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\,dud\bm{s}_{1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t^{\prime},\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+2uh\cdot\mu(t^{\prime\prime},\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(t^{\prime\prime},\bm{s}_{1})+\sigma^{2}\right]\,dud\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}\kappa_{2}^{2}}\int_{\mathcal{S}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{p(t,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\,dud\bm{s}_{1}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(v)}}}{{=}}O\left(\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\right)

with
\mu(t,\bm{s})=\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})
under model (
13
), where (i) utilizes our result in Proposition
5
for
\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]
, (ii) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and the boundedness of
\beta(t,\bm{s})
, (iii) applies the Taylor’s expansion under Assumptions
A3
and
A4
with
t^{\prime},t^{\prime\prime}
being two points between
t
and
t+uh
, (iv) absorbs the higher order terms to
O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)
, and (iv) utilizes the properties of
K
under Assumption
A5
and the positivity of
\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}|t)}{p(t,\bm{s})}
within the support
\mathcal{J}
of
p(t,\bm{s})
. Now, by Chebyshev’s inequality and our above calculations, we obtain that

\displaystyle\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}\right)+O(h^{2})

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)+O(h^{2})

as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
. As a side note, under the VC-type condition on
K
(Assumption
A5
(c)), we can apply Theorem 2 in
Einmahl and Mason (2005)
to strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\widetilde{\Xi}_{t,\zeta}(Y,T,\bm{S})\right]-\theta(t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}\right)+O(h^{2}).

I.2.2
Rate of Convergence of
Term V
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)

By direct calculations, we have that

Term V

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)\cdot p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)\cdot\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{Y_{i}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\left\{\frac{\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)\right]p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)\left[\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})-p(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\right]}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\beta(T,\bm{S})\big|T=t\right]+O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)\right\}

\displaystyle\quad\times\Bigg[\frac{O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)}{\inf\limits_{(t,\bm{s})\in\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}}p(t,\bm{s})-O_{P}\left(\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\right)}

\displaystyle\quad\quad+\frac{O_{P}\left(\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\right)}{\inf\limits_{(t,\bm{s})\in\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}}p(t,\bm{s})-O_{P}\left(\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\right)}\Bigg]

\displaystyle=\left[O(1+h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)\right]\cdot O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, where (i) utilizes our results for
Term IV
and Markov’s inequality.

Combining our results for
Term IV
and
Term V
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}+\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

I.3
Asymptotic Properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

Finally, using some similar arguments to
Section F.3
, we shall establish the asymptotic properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in (
26
). By Proposition
C.1
, we have that

\theta(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\mu(T,\bm{S})\big|T=t\right]=\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\,dF_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)=\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}=\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

where
\mu(t,\bm{s})=\bar{m}(t)+\eta(\bm{s})
.
Therefore,

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\left[Y_{i}-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-t)\cdot\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S}_{i})\right]+\int\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p},\widehat{p}_{\zeta}\right)-\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term VI}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\int\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\right]\,d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term VII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term VIII}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term IX}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term X}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term XIa}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIb}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}}_{\textbf{Term XIc}},

where
\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]
. It remains to show that the dominating
Term VI
is of order
O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
in
Section I.3.1
and the remainder terms are of order
o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
for any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
in
Section I.3.2
,
Section I.3.3
, and
Section I.3.4
, and
Section I.3.5
. We shall also derive the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in
Section I.3.6
.

I.3.1
Analysis of
Term VI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

We analyze the variance and bias of
Term VI
separately as follows. By direct calculations, we have that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\textbf{Term VI}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{4}}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{nh^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}|t)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\quad-\frac{1}{nh^{4}}\left\{\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}^{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left\{\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right\}d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h)\right]}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\left[\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left\{\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h)-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right\}d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}O\left(\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\right),

where (i) uses a change of variable and only keeps the dominating first term, (ii) leverages Taylor’s expansions, and (iii) utilizes the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{\zeta}
under Assumption
A3
as well as the fact that
\bar{p}
is lower bounded away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
. Now, by Chebyshev’s inequality, we conclude that

\displaystyle\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]\right\}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

In addition, we calculate the bias term as:

\displaystyle\mathrm{Bias}\left[\textbf{Term VI}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-(t_{1}-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{4})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\left[\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{4})\right]}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)+hu\left(\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{4})\right]\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{4})\right]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}-\frac{uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}{\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}-\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}{2\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}}{\bar{p}^{3}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}-\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}{6\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\quad+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]\left[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]}{\bar{p}^{3}(t,\bm{s}_{1})}+O(h^{4})\Bigg]

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)+hu\left(\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)+\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+\frac{u^{3}h^{3}}{6}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{4})\right]\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\int\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s})}\right]\left[\mu(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})\right]\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\left[\beta(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\left[\frac{p(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-1\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}+h^{2}\cdot B_{C,\theta}(t)+O(h^{3}),

where (i) uses a change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and (ii) applies Taylor’s expansions. Here, the complicated bias term
B_{C,\theta}(t)
is given by

\displaystyle B_{C,\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\int\frac{\left[\mu(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})\right]}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}

\displaystyle\quad\hskip 28.45274pt\times\Bigg[\frac{1}{3}\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}p(t,\bm{s})-\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})+2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\right]^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\hskip 42.67912pt+\frac{6\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})-\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}{3\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\Bigg]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\int\frac{\left[\beta(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\Bigg[\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}p(t,\bm{s})-2\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})

\displaystyle\hskip 42.67912pt+2p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\left[\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\right]^{2}-\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\Bigg]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\int\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s})}+\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{3\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}.

Under the condition that either
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
or
\bar{p}=p
, we have that

\displaystyle\int\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s})}\right]\left[\mu(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})\right]\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\quad+\int\left[\beta(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\left[\frac{p(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-1\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=0

and

\displaystyle B_{C,\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{2\kappa_{2}}\int\left[\frac{\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}-\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s})}+\frac{p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})}{3\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}\;\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\int\left[\frac{\partial^{3}}{\partial t^{3}}\mu(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}\quad\quad\text{ when }\bar{p}=p,\end{cases}

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\int\left\{\frac{3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+p(t,\bm{s})\left[\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)\right]}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\right\}\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}&\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)&\text{ when }\bar{p}=p.\end{cases}

As a result, as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, we know that

Term VI

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\int\beta(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=h^{2}B_{C,\theta}(t)+o(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

As a side note, under some VC-type condition on the kernel function
K

(
Einmahl and Mason, 2005
)
, we can strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the following uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\textbf{Term VI}\right|=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}\right);

see Theorem 4 in
Einmahl and Mason (2005)
for details.

I.3.2
Analysis of
Term VII
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

Notice that

Term VII

\displaystyle=\int\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\right]\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\leq\int\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})+\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\right|\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\int\left[\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|+\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right|\right]\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\right|p_{S}(\bm{s})\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}\right),

where (i) uses the fact that the marginal density
p_{S}
is lower bounded away from 0 within the union set
\left(\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta\right)\cup\left(\widehat{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta\right)
and (ii) leverages the boundedness of
\bar{\beta}
under Assumption
A3
as well as
\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{3,n}\right)
with
\Upsilon_{3,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. Hence,
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term VII}=o_{P}(1)
when
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\Upsilon_{5,n}=o(1)
.

I.3.3
Analyses of
Term VIII
and
Term IX
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

By Markov’s inequality, we know that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term VIII}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}\right)=o_{P}(1)

because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]^{2}\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\lesssim\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right]^{2}\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})+\left[\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(T,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\frac{\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\right]^{2}\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})+\left[\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\widehat{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\left(\mu(t+uh,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right]p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{5,n}^{2}+\Upsilon_{6,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses the change of variable
u=\frac{T-t}{h}
in the integration as well as (ii) leverages the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta}
under Assumption
A3
and the lower boundedness on
\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}}{\bar{p}},\widehat{p}
away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
by definition. As a side note, under the VC-type condition on the kernel function
K

(
Einmahl and Mason, 2005
)
and

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]=o_{P}(1),

we can strengthen the above pointwise rate of convergence to the following uniform result as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|=o_{P}(1).

Similarly, by Markov’s inequality, we have that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IX}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\max\left\{\Upsilon_{1,n},h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\right\}\right)=o_{P}(1)

because

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+(t_{1}-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)\right]^{2}\,d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{u^{2}K^{2}(u)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+hu\cdot\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right)\right]^{2}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}+h^{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}^{2}+h^{2}\Upsilon_{3,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses the change of variable
u=\frac{t_{1}-t}{h}
and (ii) leverages the boundedness of
p,p_{\zeta}
under Assumption
A3
, the lower bound on
\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}}{\bar{p}}
away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
by definition, the boundedness condition on
K
under Assumption
A5
, as well as
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{1,n}\right)
and
h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\right)
with
\Upsilon_{1,n},h\cdot\Upsilon_{3,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. In addition, if
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}(1)
and
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{h}\right)
, then the above pointwise rate of convergence can be strengthened to the uniform one as:

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T|\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}\right|=o_{P}(1).

I.3.4
Analysis of
Term X
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

We first calculate that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term X}\right|

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left|\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h}\left[\frac{\left[\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}+\frac{\left[\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})\right]^{2}\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\right\}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}\right\}}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sqrt{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}K(u)\left[\frac{\left[\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)-\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\right]^{2}}{\widehat{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}+\frac{\left[\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\right]p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt\times\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u^{2}K\left(u\right)}{\kappa_{2}^{2}}\cdot\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+hu\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]^{2}p_{T|\bm{S}}(t+uh|\bm{S})\,du\right\}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\sqrt{nh}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}(1),

where (i) uses Cauchy-Schwarz inequality and (ii) leverages our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement. As a result, by Markov’s inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term X}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{n}{h}}\cdot\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})+(T-t)\left(\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right)\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

I.3.5
Analysis of
Term XI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

By direct calculations under model (
13
) with some change of variables, we have that

Term XI
