\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{u^{2}K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}}_{\textbf{Term XIa}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du}_{\textbf{Term XIb}}

\displaystyle\hskip 9.24994pt+\underbrace{\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\right]\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du}_{\textbf{Term XIc}}.

On one hand, when
\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})=p(t,\bm{s})
, we know that
\textbf{Term XIb}=0
, and under Assumption
A6
and model (
13
),

Term XIa

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{u^{2}K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\kappa_{2}}\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta}p_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle=0.

In addition, we also have that

Term XIc

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot\left[p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\right]\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\right]\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-p_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)

by the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu}
under Assumption
A3
, the lower boundedness of
\widehat{p}
away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
, and our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement. Specifically, since
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
when
\bar{\mu}\neq\mu
and
\bar{\beta}\neq\beta
, our assumption (c) ensures that
\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
.

On the other hand, when
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
, we know from Assumptions
A4
and
A6
on
\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}
that
\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}}{\bar{p}}
is bounded away from 0 within the support and thus,

Term XIa

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{u^{2}K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\beta(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where (i) uses the facts that
\bar{p}_{\zeta}
is upper bounded while the marginal density
p_{S}
is lower bounded away from 0 within the
\zeta
-interior conditional support
\mathcal{S}(t)\ominus\zeta
and (ii) utilizes our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement to argue that
\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
if
\bar{p}\neq p,\bar{p}_{\zeta}\neq p_{\zeta}
and
\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-p_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
. In addition, we also have that

Term XIb

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\lesssim\frac{1}{h}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where we again argue from our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the theorem statement that
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
if
\bar{p}\neq p,\bar{p}_{\zeta}\neq p_{\zeta}
and
\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-p_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
. Finally, we also derive that

Term XIc

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)}{\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\right]\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\left[\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\widehat{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\bar{p}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\beta(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}\frac{uK\left(u\right)\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\right]\left[\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{3})\right]\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+2uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\left[\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+2uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})\right]\left[1+O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{6,n}\right)\right]}\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle\quad+\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{s}_{1})-\widehat{p}(u,\bm{s}_{1})\right|

\displaystyle\quad\quad\times\left|\int_{\mathbb{R}}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\left[\frac{u^{2}h^{2}}{2}\cdot\frac{\partial^{2}}{\partial t^{2}}\mu(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{3})\right]\left[p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+2uh\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})\right]}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\left[\bar{p}^{2}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+2uh\cdot\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\cdot\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\bar{p}(t,\bm{s}_{1})+O(h^{2})\right]\left[1+O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{6,n}^{2}\right)\right]}\,du\right|d\bm{s}_{1}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)+O_{P}\left(h^{2}\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\left[\Upsilon_{5,n}+\Upsilon_{6,n}\right]\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where (i) applies Taylor’s expansion and mean-value theorem for integrals as well as uses the fact that the difference between
\bar{p}
and
\widehat{p}
is small when
\sup_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\Upsilon_{6,n}\right)
, while (ii) leverages the arguments that
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot h^{2}=\sqrt{nh^{7}}\to\sqrt{c_{3}}\in[0,\infty)
and
\Upsilon_{5,n},\Upsilon_{6,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
.

I.3.6
Asymptotic Normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

For the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
, it follows from the Lyapunov central limit theorem. Specifically, we already show in
Section I.3.1
and subsequent subsections that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\left[\int\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}-\theta(t)\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+o_{P}(1)

with

\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

and
V_{C,\theta}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{C,h,t}^{2}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]=O(1)
by our calculation in
Term VI
. Then,

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]=O(1)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right|^{2+c_{1}}

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2+c_{1}}K^{2+c_{1}}\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2+c_{1}}(\bm{S}|t)\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{1}}}{n^{\frac{c_{1}}{2}}h^{1+\frac{c_{1}}{2}}\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2+c_{1}}\cdot\bar{p}^{2+c_{1}}(T,\bm{S})}\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\bar{\mathcal{S}}(t)\ominus\zeta}\frac{u^{2+c_{1}}K^{2+c_{1}}(u)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2+c_{1}}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\left[\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2+c_{1}}+\mathbb{E}|\epsilon|^{2+c_{1}}\right]}{\sqrt{(nh)^{c_{1}}}\bar{p}^{2+c_{1}}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\times p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})\,d\bm{s}_{1}du

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{(nh)^{c_{1}}}}\right)=o(1)

by the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},p
under Assumptions
A3
and
A4
, the lower boundedness of
\bar{p}
away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
, the assumption that
\mathbb{E}|\epsilon|^{2+c_{1}}<\infty
, and the requirement that
nh^{3}\to\infty
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, the Lyapunov condition holds, and we have that

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)-h^{2}B_{C,\theta}(t)\right]\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{C,\theta}(t)\right)

after subtracting the dominating bias term
h^{2}B_{C,\theta}(t)
of
\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)
that we have computed in
Term VI
. The proof is thus completed.
∎

Appendix J
Asymptotic Theory of Estimating
m(t)
Without Positivity

Under the additive confounding model (
13
), Proposition
C.1
implies that we can define the integral RA estimator (
17
) as well as the integral IPW and DR estimators of
m(t)
based on (
26
) and (
25
) as:

\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[Y_{i}+\int_{\widetilde{t}=T_{i}}^{\widetilde{t}=t}\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]\quad\text{ and }\quad\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[Y_{i}+\int_{\widetilde{t}=T_{i}}^{\widetilde{t}=t}\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right].

We prove in the following corollary that these integral estimators are consistent to
m(t)
without assuming the positivity condition.

Corollary J.1

(Consistency of estimating
m(t)
without positivity)
.

Suppose that Assumptions
A1
,
A3
,
A4
,
A5
, and
A6
are valid under the additive confounding model (
13
). In addition,
\widehat{\mu},\widehat{\beta},\widehat{p}_{\zeta},\widehat{p}
are constructed on a data sample independent of
\{(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})\}_{i=1}^{n}
. For any fixed
t\in\mathcal{T}
, we let
\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
,
\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
, and
\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})
be fixed bounded functions to which
\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{s})
,
\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)
, and
\widehat{p}(t,\bm{s})
converge under the rates of convergence as:

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{3,n}\right),\quad\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\sup_{\bm{s}\in\mathcal{S}}\left|\widehat{F}_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)-F_{\bm{S}|T}(\bm{s}|t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{4,n}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}\right),\quad\text{ and }\quad\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}\right),

where
\mathcal{T}\oplus h=\left\{u\in\mathbb{R}:\inf_{t\in\mathcal{T}}|u-t|\leq h\right\}
and
\bar{\Upsilon}_{3,n},\bar{\Upsilon}_{4,n},\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n},\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}\to 0
as
n\to\infty
. Then, as
h\to 0
and
nh^{3}\to\infty
, we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-m(t)\right|=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{3,n}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{4,n}+\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right),

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-m(t)\right|=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

If, in addition, we assume that

(a)

\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}
satisfy Assumptions
A4
and
A6
as well as
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}=o(1)
;

(b)

either
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
or
\bar{p}=p
;

(c)

\sup\limits_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]=o_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{nh}}\right)
,

then

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\left\{\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right\}d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

when
nh^{7}\to c_{3}
for some finite number
c_{3}\geq 0
, where
\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)
is defined in
Theorem 6
.
Furthermore,

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left\{\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)-h^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}B_{C,\theta}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{C,m}(t)\right)

with
V_{C,m}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]
with
B_{C,\theta}(t)
defined in
Theorem 6
.

Proof of Corollary
J.1
.

Recall from (
17
) that our integral estimator of
m(t)
is defined as:

\widehat{m}_{C}(t)=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left[Y_{i}+\int_{\widetilde{t}=T_{i}}^{\widetilde{t}=t}\widehat{\theta}_{C}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right],

where
\widehat{\theta}_{C}(t)
can be either RA (
16
), IPW (
25
), or DR (
26
) estimators. By (
15
) in Proposition
C.1
, we have that

\displaystyle\widehat{m}_{C}(t)-m(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}(Y)+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{T}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{C}(\widetilde{t})-\theta(\widetilde{t})\right]d\widetilde{t}.

(42)

Under Assumption
A3
and the condition
\mathbb{E}|\epsilon^{2+c_{1}}|<\infty
for some constant
c_{1}>0
, it is valid that
\mathrm{Var}(Y)<\infty
and
\mathrm{Var}\left[\int_{T}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]<\infty
. Thus, by Chebyshev’s inequality, we know that

\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}Y_{i}-\mathbb{E}(Y)=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{T}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Furthermore, under Assumption
A3
,
\left|\int_{T}^{t_{1}}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}-\int_{T}^{t_{2}}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right|\leq\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}|\theta(t)|\cdot|t_{1}-t_{2}|
. Together with the compactness of
\mathcal{T}
and Example 19.7 in
van der Vaart (1998)
, we also deduce that

\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}-\mathbb{E}\left[\int_{T}^{t}\theta(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]\right|=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Therefore, plugging the above rates of convergence back into (
42
), we conclude that

\displaystyle\widehat{m}_{C}(t)-m(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{C}(\widetilde{t})-\theta(\widetilde{t})\right]d\widetilde{t}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

(43)

Now, we derive the uniform rates of convergence for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
and
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)
when
\widehat{\theta}_{C}(t)
are given by
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
in (
16
) and
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)
in (
25
) respectively in
Section J.1
and
Section J.2
. We also prove the asymptotic linearity, double robustness, and asymptotic normality of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
when
\widehat{\theta}_{C}(t)
is given by
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in (
26
) in
Section J.3
.

J.1
Uniform Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)

Firstly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
when the derivative estimator is given by
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)
in (
16
). By (
43
), we have that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-m(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left[\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(\widetilde{t})-\theta(\widetilde{t})\right]d\widetilde{t}\right|+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\left|\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-\theta(t)\right|\cdot\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|\right]+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

The compactness of
\mathcal{T}
by Assumption
A3
and Markov’s inequality imply that
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|=O_{P}(1)
. Therefore, by
Theorem 6
, we conclude that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-m(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,RA}}(t)-\theta(t)\right|\cdot\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|\right]+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{3,n}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{4,n}+\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

J.2
Uniform Rate of Convergence of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)

Secondly, we derive the rate of convergence for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)
when the derivative estimator is given by
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)
in (
25
). Similar to our arguments in
Section J.1
, we derive from our results in the proof of
Theorem 6
(
Section I.2
) that

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-m(t)\right|

\displaystyle\leq\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,IPW}}(t)-\theta(t)\right|\cdot\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|\right]+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=O(h^{2})+O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{nh^{3}}}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right).

J.3
Asymptotic Properties of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

Finally, using the asymptotic properties of
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in
Theorem 6
, we shall establish the asymptotic properties of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
when the derivative function is given by
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in (
26
). Recall from the proof of
Theorem 6
(specifically,
Section I.3
) that

\displaystyle\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-\theta(t)

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\left[\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s})-\beta(t,\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}+A_{n,h}(t),

where
\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]
and
A_{n,h}(t)=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
consists of
Term VII–XI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in
Section I.3
. Then, by (
43
) and model (
13
), we know that

\displaystyle\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i_{2}}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i_{2}}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i_{2}}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T_{i_{2}},\bm{S}_{i_{2}})}\cdot\left[Y_{i_{2}}-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i_{2}})-(T_{i_{2}}-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i_{2}})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}+\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}A_{n,h}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right)

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n^{2}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i_{2}},T_{i_{2}},\bm{S}_{i_{2}};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term I}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term II}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}A_{n,h}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term III}}+O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

As for
Term II
, we know from Assumptions
A3
and
A4
that
\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right]^{2}\right\}<\infty
for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
. By the central limit theorem,

Term II

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}-\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\right).

Thus, it remains to derive the asymptotic linearity of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)
from
Term I
in
Section J.3.1
and argue that
\textbf{Term III}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
in
Section J.3.2
.

J.3.1
Analysis of
Term I
for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

Notice that the first term in
Term I
takes a form of V-statistics with a symmetric “kernel” defined as:

\displaystyle\Lambda_{t}(\bm{U}_{i_{1}},\bm{U}_{i_{2}})

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{2}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i_{2}},T_{i_{2}},\bm{S}_{i_{2}};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{1}{2}\int_{T_{i_{2}}}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i_{1}},T_{i_{1}},\bm{S}_{i_{1}};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t},

where
\bm{U}_{i}\equiv(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})
for
i=1,...,n
.
By Pascal’s rule, we know that

Term I

\displaystyle=\mathbb{P}_{n}^{2}\Lambda_{t}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=2\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\Lambda_{t}+\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)^{2}\Lambda_{t}+\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}^{2}\Lambda_{t}+\mathbb{E}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left\{\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term Ia}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term Ib}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)^{2}\left\{\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i_{2}},T_{i_{2}},\bm{S}_{i_{2}};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term Ic}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left\{\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]+\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right\}}_{\textbf{Term Id}},

where we use the shorthand notation
\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\Lambda_{t}
referring to the function
\bm{U}_{i_{1}}\mapsto\int\Lambda_{t}(\bm{U}_{i_{1}},\bm{u}_{i_{2}})\,d\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}(\bm{u}_{i_{2}})
and
\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}^{2}\Lambda_{t}=\int\int\Lambda_{t}(\bm{u}_{i_{1}},\bm{u}_{i_{2}})\,d\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}(\bm{u}_{i_{1}})d\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}(\bm{u}_{i_{2}})
.

We shall show that the dominating terms
Term Ia
and
Term Id
are of orders
O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
and
O(h^{2})
respectively, and the remainder terms
Term Ib
and
Term Ic
are of order
o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
as follows.

\bullet

Term Ia:
By our calculations in
Section I.3.1
, we know that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left[\textbf{Term Ia}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathrm{Var}\left[\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mathrm{Var}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]-\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}^{2}\Lambda_{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\frac{1}{nh^{4}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\left|t-T_{1}\right|\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]^{2}\right\}d\widetilde{t}\right]-\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}^{2}\Lambda_{t}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t+uh)}|t-t_{1}|\cdot p_{T}(t_{1})\cdot\frac{u^{2}K^{2}\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{s}_{1}|t)\cdot p(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}{\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}

\displaystyle\quad\times\left\{\left[\mu(t+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{s}_{1})-hu\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{s}_{1})\right]^{2}+\sigma^{2}\right\}d\bm{s}_{1}dudt_{1}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{nh^{3}}\right),

where (i) follows from Jensen’s inequality on the squared function and Cauchy-Schwarz inequality as
\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}g(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]^{2}\leq\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}g(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]\cdot|t-T_{1}|
for the function
g(\widetilde{t})=\mathbb{E}\left[\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]
, (ii) uses a change of variable and only keeps the dominating first term, and (iii) leverages our arguments in
Section I.3.1
. Moreover,
\mathrm{Var}\left[\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term Ia}\right]
is strictly positive as long as
\mathrm{Var}(\epsilon|T,\bm{S})>\sigma^{2}>0
. Then, by Chebyshev’s inequality, we obtain that

\displaystyle\textbf{Term Ia}=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left[\textbf{Term Ia}\right]}\right)=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

\bullet

Term Id:
By our calculation of the bias term in
Section I.3.1
, we know that

Term Id

\displaystyle=h^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}B_{C,\theta}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]+O(h^{3})=O(h^{2}),

where
B_{C,\theta}(t)
is defined in
Theorem 6
as:

\displaystyle B_{C,\theta}(t)

\displaystyle=\begin{cases}\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\int\left\{\frac{3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}p(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)+p(t,\bm{s})\left[\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)-3\frac{\partial}{\partial t}\log\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})\cdot\bar{m}^{\prime\prime}(t)\right]}{\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})}\right\}\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|t)\,d\bm{s}&\text{ when }\bar{\mu}=\mu\text{ and }\bar{\beta}=\beta,\\
\frac{\kappa_{4}}{6\kappa_{2}}\cdot\bar{m}^{(3)}(t)&\text{ when }\bar{p}=p.\end{cases}

\bullet

Term Ib:
By Chebyshev’s inequality, we have that

Term Ib

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{\mathrm{Var}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}}{n}}\right)

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{2}}}\right)=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

Here, the equality (i) above follows from the calculation that

\displaystyle\mathrm{Var}\left\{\int_{T}^{t}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{h^{3}}}\cdot\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=\int_{\mathcal{T}}\left\{\int_{t_{2}}^{t}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(t_{1})}\frac{\left(\frac{t_{1}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{t_{1}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(t_{1},\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})-(t_{1}-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}dt_{1}d\widetilde{t}\right\}^{2}p_{T}(t_{2})\,dt_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{=}}\int_{\mathcal{T}}\left\{\int_{t_{2}}^{t}\int_{\mathbb{R}}\int_{\mathcal{S}(\widetilde{t}+uh)}\frac{u\cdot K\left(u\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}_{1}|\widetilde{t})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(\widetilde{t}+uh,\bm{s}_{1})}\cdot\left[\mu(\widetilde{t}+uh,\bm{s}_{1})-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})-uh\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s}_{1})\right]d\bm{s}_{1}dud\widetilde{t}\right\}^{2}p_{T}(t_{2})\,dt_{2}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}O\left(\frac{1}{h^{2}}\right),

where (ii) utilizes a change of variable and (iii) utilizes the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p}_{\zeta},p_{T}
under Assumptions
A3
and
A4
as well as the fact that
\bar{p}
is lower bounded away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
.

\bullet

Term Ic:
Recall from Assumption
A5
(c) that
\mathcal{K}=\left\{t^{\prime}\mapsto\left(\frac{t^{\prime}-t}{h}\right)^{k_{1}}K\left(\frac{t^{\prime}-t}{h}\right):t\in\mathcal{T},h>0,k_{1}=0,1\right\}
is a bounded VC-type class of measurable functions on
\mathbb{R}
. Under Assumption
A3
and the condition that
\mathbb{E}|\epsilon^{2+c_{1}}|<\infty
, we deduce by Theorem 4 in
Einmahl and Mason (2005)
that with probability 1,

\displaystyle\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]\right|

\displaystyle=\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{|\log h|}\right)

when
\frac{|\log h|}{\log\log n}\to\infty
. Thus, by Chebyshev’s inequality,

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term Ic}

\displaystyle=\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right]^{2}\right\}}\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{|\log h|}{n}}\right)=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

Here, the last equality follows from the calculation that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left\{\left[\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]d\widetilde{t}\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\mathbb{E}\left[|t-T_{i_{1}}|^{2}\cdot\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mathbb{G}_{n}\left[\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=O\left(|\log h|\right),

where (i) applies the mean-value theorem for integrals.

As a summary for this subsection, we conclude that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term I}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\left\{\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right\}d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1).

J.3.2
Analysis of
Term III
for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

Recall from those
Term VII–XI
for
\widehat{\theta}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
in
Section I.3
that
Term III
for
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
here is given by

Term III

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}A_{n,h}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle=\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\int\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\right]d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIa}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIb}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})+(T-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIc}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})+(T-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIId}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left(\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right)d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIe}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIf}}

\displaystyle\quad+\underbrace{\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}}_{\textbf{Term IIIg}}.

We shall argue that all these terms above are of order
o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right)
respectively as follows.

\bullet

Term IIIa:
By direct calculations, we know that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IIIa}

\displaystyle=\sqrt{\frac{h^{3}}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\int\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\right]d\bm{s}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle\leq\left(\sqrt{\frac{h^{3}}{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|\right)\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\int\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\right]d\bm{s}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where in the last equality, we use the compactness of the marginal support
\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{R}
to argue that
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|t-T_{i}|=O_{P}(1)
for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
and utilize our derivations in
Section I.3.2
to obtain that
\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\int\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\left[\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\right]d\bm{s}\right|=O_{P}\left(\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}\right)
.

\bullet

Term IIIb:
Notice that
Term IIIb
can be written in a form of V-statistics. Specifically,

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IIIb}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

with
\bm{Z}_{i}(\widetilde{t})=\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})\right]
. Note that the random variables
\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{j}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{j}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}
and
\int_{T_{k}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{\ell}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{\ell}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}
are dependent and have a nonzero covariance when any of
i,j,k,\ell
coincides. Thus, the variance of

\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

involves a sum of
O(n^{3})
nonzero terms; see,
e.g.
, Section 6.1 in
Lehmann (1999)
for detailed explanations. By Chebyshev’s inequality,

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IIIb}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\mathrm{Var}\left[\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

Here, the last equality follows from the calculations that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\mathbb{E}\left[(t-T_{i_{1}})^{2}\left|\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sup_{t^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[(t-T_{i_{1}})^{2}\left|\bm{Z}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sup_{t^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-t^{\prime}}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T_{i}-t^{\prime}}{h}\right)}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t^{\prime})}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|t^{\prime})}{\bar{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]^{2}\left[Y_{i}-\bar{\mu}(t^{\prime},\bm{S}_{i})-(T_{i}-t^{\prime})\cdot\bar{\beta}(t^{\prime},\bm{S}_{i})\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-\bar{p}(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}^{2}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (i) applies the mean-value theorem for integrals with
t^{\prime}
lying between
t,T_{i_{1}}\in\mathcal{T}
, (ii) uses the compactness of
\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{R}
, and (iii) utilizes our derivations in
Section I.3.3
.

\bullet

Term IIIc:
Analogous to our arguments for
Term IIIb
, we write
Term IIIc
in terms of V-statistics and deduce that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IIIc}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\left(\mathbb{P}_{n}-\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\right)\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})+(T-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

Here,
\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i}(\widetilde{t})=\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})+(T_{i}-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right]
, and the last equality above follows from some similar calculations as:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\left(\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\left\{\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\right]\right\}\right)^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[(t-T_{i_{1}})^{2}\left|\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})-\mathbb{E}\left[\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})\right]\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\sup_{t^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\left[(t-T_{i_{1}})^{2}\left|\bar{\bm{Z}}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})\right|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{t^{\prime}\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)^{2}K^{2}\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}^{2}\cdot\bar{p}^{2}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})+(T_{i}-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right]^{2}\right\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}+h^{2}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{1,n}^{2}+h^{2}\bar{\Upsilon}_{3,n}^{2}\right)=o_{P}(1),

where (iv) again leverages our derivations in
Section I.3.3
.

\bullet

Term IIId:
Similar to our arguments for
Term IIIb
, we also write
Term IIId
in terms of V-statistics and utilize Markov’s inequality to deduce that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term IIId}

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i}}^{t}\mathbb{P}_{n}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})+(T-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right]\right\}d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle:=\frac{1}{n^{\frac{3}{2}}}\sum_{i_{1}=1}^{n}\sum_{i_{2}=1}^{n}\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\bm{V}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sqrt{n}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\bm{V}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right|\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}(1).

Here,
\bm{V}_{i}(\widetilde{t})=\frac{\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T_{i}-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}_{i}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T_{i},\bm{S}_{i})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})+(T_{i}-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S}_{i})\right]\right]
, and the last equality above follows from the following calculation as:

\displaystyle\sqrt{n}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left|\int_{T_{i_{1}}}^{t}\bm{V}_{i_{2}}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\sqrt{n}\cdot\mathbb{E}\left[|t-T_{i_{1}}|\cdot\left|\bm{V}_{i_{2}}(t^{\prime})\right|\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{n}\cdot\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})+(T-\widetilde{t})\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right]\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\sqrt{nh}\cdot\sup\limits_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\Bigg\{\left[\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-p_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup\limits_{|u-t|\leq h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\quad\times\left[\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iv)}}}{{=}}o_{P}(1),

where applies the mean-value theorem for integrals with
t^{\prime}
lying between
t,T_{i_{1}}\in\mathcal{T}
, (ii) uses the compactness of
\mathcal{T}\subset\mathbb{R}
, (iii) utilizes our derivations in
Section I.3.4
, and (iv) leverages our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the corollary statement.

\bullet

Term IIIe, Term IIIf, and Term IIIg:
On one hand, when
\bar{p}(t,\bm{s})=p(t,\bm{s})
for all
(t,\bm{s})\in\mathcal{T}\times\mathcal{S}
, we know from our calculations in
Section I.3.5
that
\textbf{Term IIIe}=\textbf{Term IIIf}=0
and

Term IIIg

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(i)}}}{{\leq}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|\right)\cdot\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{p(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(ii)}}}{{\lesssim}}\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|\right)\cdot\frac{1}{h}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]

\displaystyle\stackrel{{\scriptstyle\text{(iii)}}}{{=}}O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{h}\left(\bar{\Upsilon}_{5,n}+\bar{\Upsilon}_{6,n}\right)\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

where (i) utilizes the mean-value theorem for integrals, (ii) follows from our derivations in
Section I.3.5
, and (iii) applies Markov’s inequality to
\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|
as well as leverages our assumption (c) on the doubly robust rate of convergence in the corollary statement. Specifically, since
\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+h\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=O_{P}(1)
for any
t\in\mathcal{T}
when
\bar{\mu}\neq\mu
and
\bar{\beta}\neq\beta
, our assumption (c) ensures that
\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh}}\right)
.

On the other hand, when
\bar{\mu}=\mu
and
\bar{\beta}=\beta
, we again know from our calculations in
Section I.3.5
that

Term IIIe

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|\right)

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)^{2}K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}+\int\left[\widehat{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\beta}(t,\bm{S})-\beta(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

Term IIIf

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|\right)\cdot\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{h^{2}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\left[\mu(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-\widehat{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(\frac{1}{h}\cdot\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-\mu(t,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right),

and

Term IIIg

\displaystyle\leq\left(\frac{1}{n}\sum_{i=1}^{n}|T_{i}-t|\right)\cdot\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\mbox{$\mathrm{P}$}\left\{\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)}{h^{2}\kappa_{2}}\left[\frac{\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\widehat{p}(T,\bm{S})}-\frac{\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\right]\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]\right\}\right|

\displaystyle=O_{P}\left(h^{2}\left[\sup_{t\in\mathcal{T}}\left|\left|\widehat{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)-p_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)\right|\right|_{L_{2}}+\sup_{u\in\mathcal{T}\oplus h}\left|\left|\widehat{p}(u,\bm{S})-p(u,\bm{S})\right|\right|_{L_{2}}\right]\right)

\displaystyle=o_{P}\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{nh^{3}}}\right).

As a summary for this subsection, we conclude that
\sqrt{nh^{3}}\cdot\textbf{Term III}=o_{P}(1)
.

J.3.3
Asymptotic Normality of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)

For the asymptotic normality of
\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)
, it follows from the Lyapunov central limit theorem. Specifically, we already show in
Section J.3.1
and
Section J.3.2
that

\displaystyle\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left[\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\left\{\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)+\sqrt{h^{3}}\int\left[\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})-\beta(\widetilde{t},\bm{s})\right]\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{s}|\widetilde{t})\,d\bm{s}\right\}d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}+o_{P}(1)

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right]+o_{P}(1)

with

\phi_{C,h,t}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)=\frac{\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-t}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|t)}{\sqrt{h}\cdot\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(t,\bm{S})-(T-t)\cdot\bar{\beta}(t,\bm{S})\right]

and
V_{C,m}(t)=\mathbb{E}\left[\left\{\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}^{2}\right]=O(1)
by our calculation of
Term Ia
in
Section J.3.1
. Then,

\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathrm{Var}\left\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y_{i},T_{i},\bm{S}_{i};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}=O(1)

and

\displaystyle\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mathbb{E}\left|\frac{1}{\sqrt{n}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\phi_{C,h,\widetilde{t}}\left(Y,T,\bm{S};\bar{\mu},\bar{\beta},\bar{p},\bar{p}_{\zeta}\right)d\widetilde{t}\right]\right|^{2+c_{4}}

\displaystyle\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{c_{4}}h^{c_{4}+2}}}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left|\int_{T_{1}}^{t}\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)K\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\kappa_{2}\cdot\bar{p}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[Y-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]d\widetilde{t}\right|^{2+c_{4}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\Bigg\{\frac{1}{\sqrt{n^{c_{4}}h^{c_{4}+2}}}\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\Bigg[|t-T_{1}|^{2+c_{4}}

\displaystyle\quad\times\sup_{\widetilde{t}\in\mathcal{T}}\frac{\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)^{2+c_{4}}K^{2+c_{4}}\left(\frac{T-\widetilde{t}}{h}\right)\cdot\bar{p}_{\zeta}^{2+c_{4}}(\bm{S}|\widetilde{t})}{\kappa_{2}^{2+c_{4}}\cdot\bar{p}^{2+c_{4}}(T,\bm{S})}\cdot\left[\mu(T,\bm{S})+\epsilon-\bar{\mu}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})-(T-\widetilde{t})\cdot\bar{\beta}(\widetilde{t},\bm{S})\right]^{2+c_{4}}d\widetilde{t}\Bigg]\Bigg\}

\displaystyle=O\left(\sqrt{\frac{1}{n^{c_{4}}h^{2+c_{4}}}}\right)=o(1)

by the upper boundedness of
\mu,\bar{\mu},p
under Assumptions
A3
and
A4
, the upper boundedness of the kernel function under Assumption
A5
(c), the lower boundedness of
\bar{p}
away from 0 around the support
\mathcal{J}
, the assumption that
\mathbb{E}|\epsilon|^{2+c_{1}}<\infty
for some constant
c_{1}\geq 1
, and the requirement that
nh^{3}\to\infty
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, the Lyapunov condition holds, and we have that

\sqrt{nh^{3}}\left\{\widehat{m}_{\mathrm{C,DR}}(t)-m(t)-h^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}B_{C,\theta}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]\right\}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{\to}}\mathcal{N}\left(0,V_{C,m}(t)\right)

after subtracting the dominating bias term
h^{2}\cdot\mathbb{E}_{T_{1}}\left[\int_{T_{1}}^{t}B_{C,\theta}(\widetilde{t})\,d\widetilde{t}\right]
that we have computed in
Term Id
in
Section J.3.1
. The proof is thus completed.
∎
